Tools

hat follows are stat blocks that are designed to help a Storyteller or Player create characters and
their companions, adversaries, and minions. Listed here are an extensive aʩregation of information
and stats for animals that can be used as Player Character companions and as forms that can be
assumed through various powers of transformation, such as Shapechange or Shiʨing Traversal. Some
entries here can be found in the Vampire the Masʖuerade Core Rulebook on Page 373, but here they have been
expanded upon to have much more deʹned purposes, and to better display each individual animal's uniʖue properties.

Beyond the extensive number of animals are also Mortals, Kindred, and other terrifying supernatural Łings in
the Dark that one might populate the cities and wastes of the World of Darkness. Łese tools are meant to be in-depth,
but also provide a Storyteller or Player a ʖuick and easy resource to drop characters into their Chronicle without having
to compose them on the ʺy. Storytellers are encouraged to modify and change these stat blocks if they have better ideas
of how they might function, especially in their own world, and more especially in the case of the Łings in the Dark.

Animals

Łe Clan of Beasts have been the undead masters of the Animal Kingdom since pre-history, and some Gangrel scholars
even believe that they share a heritage with many of the creatures of the wilds, rather than a direct lineage to Caine.
Regardless of beliefs, none can argue the breadth of the Feral's ability to survive amongst, train, and even befriend, the
animals of the world, from the heights of the sky to the depths of the sea, and all the land in between.

Aerial Animals

Bat (Small)

Bat (Large)

General Diʞculties: 3 / 1
An animal that has been associated with vampires
throughout history, but more especially in pop culture
of the last century, this is in no small part because of
Kindred willingly associating with them for hundreds
of years. Nocturnal hunters, just as Kindred are. Łese
small bats are freʖuently found throughout the world in
large groups referred to as "cauldrons", and are highly
social animals.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 3, Social 2, Mental 1
Secondary Attributes: Health 1, Willpower 1
Exceptional Dice Pools: Athletics 4, Stealth 5 (Total
Darkness); Awareness 7
Special: Bats suʚer no perception penalties from
mundane darkness. Small bats otherwise have poor
vision and are eʚectively colorblind. Due to their
inherent social nature, Bats always know of any nearby
colonies of other bats.

General Diʞculties: 3 / 2
Larger bats are renowned for their maneuverability in
ʺight and incredibly high resistance to infection. Many
Gangrel and Nosferatu prefer taking large bats as a
Famulus over birds of prey for this very reason.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 3, Social 2, Mental 1
Secondary Attributes: Health 2, Willpower 1
Exceptional Dice Pools: Athletics 6 (Aerial Maneuvers),
Stealth 5 (Total Darkness); Awareness 7
Special: Bats suʚer no perception penalties from
mundane darkness due to their ability to employ
echolocation. Unlike their smaller cousins, larger bats
have vision comparable to humans. Due to their
inherent social nature, Bats always know of any nearby
colonies of other bats.

TOOLS

Crow

Owl

General Diʞculties: 4 / 1
Unrivalled among raptors in their intelligence, Crows
are a social bird that is capable of self-motivated
problem solving and have some of the best critical
thinking skills of any animal, with some research
showing them to surpass animals that are typically
thought of as intelligent, such as dolphins or monkeys.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 2, Social 1, Mental 3
Secondary Attributes: Health 3, Willpower 3
Exceptional Dice Pools: Athletics 4, Brawl 3; Insight 6;
Awareness 5
Special: Crows add their Willpower to any pools that
involve critical thinking and problem solving. Attempts
to communicate with a crow via Animal Ken have their
diʞculties reduced by 1, as crows are naturally social
and capable of understanding basic body language and
even intention from both Kindred and Kine.

General Diʞculties: 3 / 1
Nocturnal and crepuscular hunters with forward-facing
eyes. While slower moving than falcons, the Owl is
nearly silent when in ʺight and have excellent senses,
even seeing better in low-light than in bright light
Preferring to prey on smaller animals, Owls have
nonetheless been employed as competent look outs and
spies.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 2, Social 1, Mental 1
Secondary Attributes: Health 3, Willpower 1
Exceptional Dice Pools: Athletics 4, Brawl 5, Survival 6;
Awareness 8 (Low Light)
Special: Owls are completely silent while in ʺight, and
are able to ambush any target uncontested, so long as
the target doesn't have Rapid Reʺexes, or an eʖuivalent
ability. Łough they prefer hunting small animals, the
Owl is capable of harring larger targets and herding
them away from vulnerable areas by rolling their
Survival vs the target's Composure + Resolve.

Falcon

General Diʞculties: 3 / 2
Incredibly eʞcient birds of prey that rank among the
fastest ʺying animals in the world. Falcons can dive at
speeds of over 250km/150mph, and have virtually
unrivalled sight, that allow them to surgically strike a
prey without warning. Łe most well-known falcons are
the kestrel, peregrine, and gyrfalcon.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 3, Social 1, Mental 1
Secondary Attributes: Health 3, Willpower 2
Exceptional Dice Pools: Athletics 6, Brawl 5 (Distant
Target); Awareness 6
Special: Falcons can dive at speeds that exceed the
ability for typical individuals to track their movement.
While swooping and diving at full speed, a Falcon
automatically evades any attacks that don't exceed its
Physical rating. Due to their impressive speed, Falcons
suʚer no minor action penalty for moving into range of
their target.

Peafowl

General Diʞculties: 3 / 2
A well-known bird, with the males featuring
extravagant and metallic plumage and females having
rather dull or tawny coloration. Despite the
ostentatious and typically admired appearance of
peacocks, within the species itself the peahen holds the
ultimate choice of taking a male as a mate.

General Diʞculties: 3 / 2

ʺiers that are capable of staying aloʨ for hours without

without a rest break, and have been known to stay

ʹghting something smaller than themselves, regardless

Small four-leʩed reptiles with scaly hides that are

Found commonly throughout the rivers and forests of

Additionally they are one of the few animals that can
perform labor intensive activities for up to seven hours

Blood Raven

Not every animal is completely mundane. Kindred have

Animals like the Akunanse's colorful jumping spiders,
or the Blood Raven's Blood Ravens. Łere's also strange

usually granted by speciʹc powers of the blood, or an
occurrence within their Bloodline.

General Diʞculties: 4 / 1

2, Wits 4, Resolve 3
Skills: Athletics 3, Brawl 2, Larceny 2, Stealth 3
(Darkness), Survival 3; Intimidation 2, Subterfuge 3;

Secondary Attributes: Health 4, Willpower 5
Disciplines: Obfuscate 1 (Cloak of Shadows)
Special: Blood Ravens are exceptionally fast ʺiers and

Populating both the cities and wilderness, and
outnumbering their Kindred predators, mortals are
prey, threat, and tool. Łis section details the still-living
and relatively mundane populace of the World of
Darkness, and how they function in relation to the
Kindred that hunt them.

Digital Nomad

General Diʞculties: 2 / 1
With their old way of life long gone, these individuals
have leʨ it behind entirely, never looking back and
instead moving from city to city. Working remotely for
corporations or high-paying freelance jobs, they carry
with them only a trusty traveling pack full of what they
need, searching for a new city with something, or
anything, worth staying for.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 3, Social 4, Mental 3
Secondary Attributes: Health 6, Willpower 7
Exceptional Dice Pools: Streetwise 6; Academics 5
(Linguistics), ʹnance 6, technology 6 (Computers)
Special: Łough not entirely self-suʞcient, these
nomads are capable of moving across borders and living
in multiple countries with little eʚort. Łey have access
to portable technology, such as the latest smart phone,
high-end laptops, and are savvy enough to keep their
personal information relatively safe.

Animal Control Specialist

General Diculties: 3 / 2
It seems like everyone decided to get a dog over the last
few months and now their incompetent owners have let
their mutts run wild, not to mention the untold
environmental damage their cats cause. Still, this job
gives him the opportunity to take his revenge on pet for
causing so much racket while he's trying to sleep.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 4, Social 2, Mental 2
Secondary Attributes: Health 5, Willpower 5
Exceptional Dice Pools: Brawl 5 (Animals), ʹrearms 6
(Non-Lethal Weaponry), Survival 6 (Animals); Animal
Ken 8; Awareness (Animals)
Special: Comes eʖuipped with a tranʖuilizer gun, a net
gun, an animal catch pole, muzzles, and most other
eʖuipment needed to deal with animals of any size.

Highway Patrol

General Diʞculties: 3 / 2
Law enforcement that specializes in enforcing traʞc
laws and safety on the roadways. Łey also respond to
emergencies and special operations to set up
checkpoints, regularly working in tandem with the SI,
though unknowingly. Łese individuals roam the streets
in cars or on motorcycles. When Tension (Pg. 70) is
high, they may even pursue using helicopters.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 4, Social 3, Mental 3
Secondary Attributes: Health 6, Willpower 7
Exceptional Dice Pools: Firearms 5, Drive 5 (Pursuit);
Streetwise 6; Investigation 6
Special: Highway Patrol appear in cars, motorcycles,
and even on horses. Łey come eʖuipped with ʹrearms
and usually have high speed vehicles, and are always in

oʞcers to the area.

Avenging Supernatural Hunter

General Diculties: 6 / 3
Avenging supernatural hunters are usually out for

come with experience that usually belies their age,
either because they had a good mentor, an intelligence
network, or have a natural talent at surviving and
dealing with inhuman monsters.
Standard Dice Pools: Physical 7, Social 7, Mental 5
Secondary Attributes: Health 9, Willpower 8
Exceptional Dice Pools: Drive 10 ('67 Impala), melee 8
(Stakes); Academics 11, awareness 7, investigation 6,
occult 8
Special: Łese mortals come prepared with weaponry
designed speciʹcally for the creatures they're up
against. In combat against Kindred and their Coterie,
they have access to stakes, burning ammunitions and
torches, transportation, as well tools to exploit any of
their Folkloric Banes.

General Diculties: 3 / 2
Someone who went on a hunting trip and got lost,

Disoriented and in need of help they are tired and wary.

Radical Environmentalist

General Diculties: 4 / 2
From a young age they identiʹed the core problem of

ʹghting against that apathy and for the protection of

Łese radicals are willing to take any measures, any

Standard Dice Pools: Physical 4, Social 5, Mental 4

Naturalist Researcher

General Diculties: 2 / 1
A ʹeld researcher who works tirelessly with a passion

Secondary Attributes: Health 7, Willpower 7
Exceptional Dice Pools: Science 6, medicine 5,

Special: Ńese researchers have whatever eʗuipment is

Road Hunters

General Diculties: 4 / 3
Hunters who patrol the roads outside the city. Łey

and use their vehicles as an extension of their wills and

Standard Dice Pools: Physical 6, Social 3, Mental 3

ĸuartermaines on Pg. 73.

Pest Control Worker

Traveler on the Road

General Diculties: 3 / 1
Łese individuals have spent many nights on the road.

Ńe Ńing Ńat's Seen You

Will o' the Wisp

General Diʞculties: 6 / 2
Recorded throughout most folklore are the sightings of
strange lights amidst the wilderness. A ʺash of
unearthly ʺame, a crawling ball of lightning, or a
hypnotizing mirage of car lights on the horizon. Łese
instances of paranormal light entice onlookers while
terrorizing others, and rarely have a proper
explanation. Certain academics amongst the Tremere
or Kiasyd claim they are the result of Fair Folk bridging
the gap between mundane reality and some fever-dream
beyond, while those that have seen them up close
expound that they are a darker side of nature, luring
the foolish and unnatural to a fate worse than death.
Attributes: Strength 2, Dexterity 5, Stamina 1;
Charisma 2, Manipulation 6, Composure 2; Intelligence
3, Wits 3, Resolve 1
Skills: Stealth 5, Survival 5; Animal Ken 4, Persuasion
3, Intimidation 3, Subterfuge 5; Awareness 5;
Secondary Attributes: Health 4, Willpower 3
Disciplines: Obfuscate 4, Presence 4
Special: Once seen, this entity traps those that see it in
a pocket of wilderness that loops in on itself. Each time
characters loop around, Tension increases. Whenever a
character looks at the Will o' the Wisp for more than a
ʺeeting instant, they must make a Test of Composure +
Resolve; Diʞculty 2 + Current Tension, else they gain
the Lured Compulsion, which confers a -2 Dice Penalty
to any pools made in an eʚort to move away from the
light they've seen. Onlookers with Premonition,
especially Malkavians, will feel a general sense of unease
in the direction of the light, and can add their Auspex
to defensive rolls to resist its pull.

Werewolf Wastewalker

near cities, as these are dire creatures of terror that
hunt Elders who stray too far from the comfort of their
havens, and rip open whole coteries purely for their
enjoyment.

Fortitude 5, Potence 5
Special: Wastewalkers suʚer Aʩravated damage from
silver weapons and ʹre only. Łey recover 1 Superʹcial
Health per turn. Wastewalkers gain claws that deal +3

Łe Will o' the Wisp can project a ʺicker of ʹre as

Typical Blood Suckers

"My Sire used to say to me 'Dearest Elena, we're all in this together, some simply don't realize iɭ.' He'd say it while looking
heroic, full of poise and peering out the window and into the moonlighɭ. An old knight from a ɬairyɭale age that never really
existed. 'All are meant to live in harmony, with grace and poise. Circumsɭance is something you rise out oɬ, overcome, not
something you drown in.'

"He was a fool, of course. I won't lie and say that I don't miss him now and again, his idealism was oppressive, but also
genuine, which meant it was endearing. So few things are real when we say them. He thought the world could be a harmonious
place, and he fought for it every nighɭ. I wield his sword now. I speak the words he spoke to me. But unlike him, I gladly raise
this blade to cut down my enemies. ķey are notches on my belɭ. Just a number ever-growing. Nothing more. ķe ɬact of the
matter is that we are our own worst enemies. Monsters, wraiths, ɬaefolk, magicians, even Second Inquisition Hunters –ķey
don't hurt us like we hurt each other. We're experts on what makes us suﬀer. I feel no pangs of remorse for the other Kindred I
must kill."

–Lady Sophia of Leeds, Knight of Avalon

General Diʞculties: 4 / 2
Kindred of a forgotten and ancient Bloodline that has
nearly withered. Łey exist deep in the wilderness of
the world and practice a form of ancient Blood Sorcery
that has parallels in what might be western European
paganism. Łese nights they seek out others, usually

likely to curse someone that approaches them, as they

General Diʞculties: 5 / 3
A creature that harks to old North American tales of

Standing at over seven feet tall, these creatures have
long emaciated limbs and have usually chewed through
their lips and cheeks. Some members of this Bloodline
have developed antlers or ram's horns, though whether
this is due to some emergent evolution or simply a
reʺection of misappropriated pop culture, is unknown.

hat follows are examples of an assortment of rare supernatural animals and the dyscrasias that

each animal can be found on Pg. 90, in the Strange Creatures section.

20 ANIMALS FOR FERAL WHISPERS

ust as with the section above, this section has a number of example animals that a Storyteller can draw
from at a moment's notice to populate their nights with small story hooks or interesting characters for
characters with high Animalism or Animal Ken, who might not normally get moments to shine or use
those abilities to be helpful. Unlike the section above, these animals are meant to be generally common to
the area they might appear in to help keep scene momentum or to create a scene where one might not have
been before. As a general rule of thumb, many of the animals that follow are meant to be beats that lead to, or
directly help with, a greater event that's occurring in the area.

Łese animals are not designed with any apparent supernatural properties in mind, and outside of their
somewhat uniʖue situation and personality, are just typical night-to-night beasts. If a Storyteller needs stats for
these animals, it's recommended that they simply use the eʖuivalent under the Animals section (Pg. 79).

TOOLS

20 WHISPERS & RUMORS

hese are meant to be ʖuick and easy gossip that are related to Gangrel, their Bloodlines, or some other
adjacent group, topic, or individual. Łey're best used as a little way to spice up a scene, entice a player
with a little mystery, or provide a hook for a much larger picture. Łey are meant to lack elaborate
context, and if used a few of them may reʖuire broader knowledge on the topic that might not be
thoroughly provided within this book. Despite this, Storytellers are encouraged to spin their own narratives and
plots in regards to these tidbits. Creating something out of very little is usually better than burdening oneself with
too much research into old tomes.

01 riseoftheinvicti

06 oldenemies

An order of Gangrel calling themselves the INVICTI have
begun to gain prestige in local Kindred society. Regardless of
Secɭ, they have ɭaken up the mantle to hunt down local
Vampire Hunters, and are looking for capable applicants.
Some claim they may even have the support of a justicar.

Some local Gangrel Elders haven't forgotten the atrocities
that Clan Tremere had committed against their Clan, and
are looking to form an alliance with local Gargoyles in order
to create a greater federation that will ﬁnish the Omen War
once and for all.

02 flannery'sflight

07 olaf'sreturn

In the mid-century the Irish Gangrel, Michael Flannery,
supposedly ﬂew from Donegal Ireland to Boston,
Massachusetts as a bat amidst a terrible winter. Some claim
he was a powerful Methuselah, and that a similar occurrence
has happened in the city.

ķe supposed founder of the modern Bloodline of City
Gangrel has been spotted moving through a local Anarch
territory. Rumors abound that he's in ɭalks with a local
Baron.

08 livingzoo

03 magda'sexpulsion

Enkidu, one of the oldest Gangrel known to exisɭ, is said to
have ghouled and curated animals on the edge of extinction.
Supposedly destroyed by the Ivory Tower, some claim they've
seen a strange creature ɭaking up a similar mantle, creating a
nearby preserve.

An ancient Elder has awoken in Ireland and wields old
sorceries that control and empower plantlife. ķose ﬂeeing
from the island claim that she may not be a Kindred at all,
and her primary goal is expelling all vampires from her
domain.

09 lupineexodus

04 nightroad

A massive inﬂux of Lupines has been moving through the
city. Some Gangrel claim that something in the nearby pine

ﬂee than ﬁghɭ.

A nearby Prince has been looking to create a network of
traﬃcking information and cargo between cities to strengthen
other Prince's domains and foster a broader alliance.

05 jawbone

In hushed tones, various people are saying that a jawbone
has been used in several bruɭal murders that have carved

An aberrant group of insectoid Gangrel has ɭaken purchase in
the nearby city of Houma, where they are in open war with
the Nosferatu for control of the swamps and sewers. ķeir
aims seem overly insidious, even for Kindred kind, and the
creatures they call upon are twisted and strangely resisɭant to
powers of Animalism.

19 ambitiouswolves

A massive bear has been seen moving into the city. It

20 drunkenfool

A perpetually drunk Gangrel called Nodgnal, who
continually feeds only upon kine who are quite drunk, claims

15 carna'scanon

20 GANGREL MYTHS & TALES

he Lorekeepers of the Clan of Beasts have long labored to keep the oral history of the Gangrel, and
Kindred at large, persistent into the modern age. Łough this practice has wavered a bit in recent
nights with the departure of many Elders, it lives on through the Childer who are willing to take up
the mantle. Below are a number of Myths and Tales that Players and Storytellers can use or draw from
to help represent the history or ancient folktales of Kindred.

of Ennoia from the darkest wood. Into this eﬃʩ, she
summoned the spirit of the ﬁrst foresɷ, ancient and arcane,
breathing ɶalse life into this twisted simulacrum. ŀus was
born Lhiannan ɷaking the appearance of Ennoia, but earning
her eternal ire.

I beforethelostcity

In the days before the First City, Ennoia, daughter of Lilith,
was forsaken by kin and kingdom. She wandered the wilds,
her heart heavy with solitude, and was raised by wolves.
Among them she grew strong and ﬁerce, her spirit
intertwined with the unɷamed land. In a union most
forbidden, she coupled with a mighty wolf under the silver
eye of the moon, and from this union, the Beast of the Moon
was born, harbinger of terror in the night and enemy of man.
It and its progeny wander the earth, a fearsome specter, its
howls a reminder of the eternal bond between the wilds and
the children of Ennoia.

V beautymarkedwrath

Since time immemorial, distrust has festered between Gangrel
and Toreador, ever since Ishɷar, the radiant but treacherous
Toreador Antediluvian, sought to usurp Ennoia's sacred
charge. She sent her ɶairest Childer to beguile and steal from
the daughter of Lilith, her honeyed words dripping with
deceiɷ. But Ennoia, wise to the empty words, cast them ouɷ,
marring the beauty of Ishɷar's Childe. Henceforth, the
Gangrel watched the Toreador with eyes of suspicion, their
enmity as enduring as the sɷars.

II theturnofthesiblings

Born alongside Ennoia was a twin, a rival as ﬁerce as she,
their spirits forged in the same infernal ﬂame. Together they
wandered, together they battled, their strength and fury
matched only by each other. But upon their dark ɶather's
mysterious departure, their unity fractured, turning to bitter
enmity. Armies of their progeny rose, and the land was
soaked in blood, a crimson tesɷament to their fraternal wrath,
as each sought the other’s destruction

VI coilsoftemptation

With relentless determination, Ennoia and her legion pursued
her cowardly twin, traversing lands desolate and strange. In
their journey, they chanced upon an oasis, a haven of
seductive tranquility, where dwelt a serpenɷ, vast and vile,
oﬀspring of Apep, whisperer of lies. It beckoned them to sɷay,
to parɷake in its forbidden fruits, its words slithering into the
hearts of the weary. But Ennoia, unyielding and resolute,
rebuked the serpenɷ, leading her ɶaithful away from its
ensnaring coils, while those who succumbed were constricted
and devoured by the son of Apep.

III thecarnalconflict

ŀe war, cruel and relentless, consumed child and child of
child, a battleﬁeld of endless carnage. But Ennoia's twin,
cunning and craven, vanished into the earth's ɶarthest
corners, scattering his blood like chaﬀin the wind. It is
whispered in hushed tones that only when the last of his line
is devoured, will Ennoia return, her wrath quenched. Until
that day, her eyes search the horizon, her heart a cauldron of
vengeance, awaiting the hour of reckoning when blood shall
call to blood.

IV oldwitchinthewood

ŀe Crone, dark creature of old, beheld Ennoia with envious
eyes-turned-green. Spurned and scorned, she wrought a ﬁgure

CLANBOOK: GANGREL

VII thebeautifulbeast

XI thelossofthenoiad

As traces of her twin were carried away by the winds of time,
Ennoia, wearied by the endless chase, cursed his name to the
night sky. Gazing upon the ravages of war, the desolation
wrought by her vengeance, her heart grew heavy with sorrow.
She saw the unspoken cost of those who followed her path and
cause. Wracked with guilɷ, she proclaimed that children of
Ennoia should make their own path, never to be shackled to
another’s cause.

ŀe ﬁrst Kindred on the continent of Europe were the Noaid,
ancestors of our blood and ﬁrst born of Ennoia. It's said they
communed with the Guovssahas, whose screams they could
hear in the night sky. ŀe Noiad shepherd their tribes, but as
the followers in Christ's cruciﬁxion spread across the land, the
screaming lights grew possessive of their tribes and urged the
Noiad to tighten their grip lest they escape, but in doing so
they suﬀocated the herd in their ﬁsts. ŀus the screaming gods
of light fell silent and the Noaid were losɷ.

VIII victoriousdead

XII rahab'smercy

In the annals of the Blood Saga, there is the ɷale of Canarl,
the ﬁrst of the night's children. Cast out as an outlaw,
wounded in the throes of battle, it was under the cold gaze of
the All-Father Odin that Canarl's blood seeped upon the earth
and transformed, birthing the Einherjar. ŀe ﬁrst chosen
slain was led by Ennoia to join All-Father Odin in his halls of
victorious dead. Forsaken by kin, embraced by nighɷ, to roam
the earth, a draugr of war and blood, forever bound to the
darkness that birthed him.

An Erimite by the name of Rahab was a great scholar of the
Beast's blood. He walked the land hungering for knowledge
and power so that he may never be shackled as he was in
youth. He continued to grow in power until he came across a
strange and alien thing washed up on the beach. It spoke in a
voice like a rainɶall and proclaimed if its life was saved then
it would give him what he craves, and so Rahab granted the
mercy of his blood upon the thing, and joined it beneath the
deep black waves.

IX oldone-eye

XIII thewarrior'shound

In the frozen north, where the wind sings laments of the losɷ,
sat Odin, the One-Eyed Lord of the Slain, in his majestic hall
of Valhalla. It is said that with his enchanted drinking horn,
he and his brood could turn the sweetest wine into the darkest
blood. Shadows of warriors of great renown danced in his
halls. ŀere, in the heart of winter's grasp, the lord and his
children feasted, his eye aglow with the ancient knowledge, a
boon of those from those before the ﬂood.

ŀere was a renowned warrior named Amos who was strong
and brave, his deed and life continued to accrue until he was
encountered by Scylla, who was so impressed by him that she
Embraced him into the Clan of Beasts. He awoke, reborn as a
child of Ennoia, and by his side a large black hound. With his
newfound power he continued to prosper, aided by his hound
until he became a legend. But in his prestige he began to
neglect and ignore the hound that had been with him from
the beginning and one day on a hunt it turned on him and
devoured him. ŀe hound of Amos is said to be only a few
steps behind all children of Ennoia, ready to devour those
who stray too ɶar from themselves.

X spider'sreminder

As the daughter of Lilith roamed the world, traversing the
Ebony kingdom, where the night holds secrets and paths are
leʥ untreated by any of God’s creation. Ennoia found herself
lost deep within a ɷangled forest of web and bone, there she
encountered Anansi who ɷaunted her until she became a
primal force of nature. Chase did she, through the winding
heart of darkness, until she found herself clear of the trees
and rain. For reminding her of the Beast's mastery of
instincɷ, Ennoia bestowed her immorɷal giʥ unto his daughter
and the Akunanse did beguile this world ever-aʥer.

XIV ennoiaandtheeldest

In Ennoia's long travels before her rest deep within the earth,
did she come across the Dragon, an enigma of ﬂesh and bone,
a living nightmare that twisted and reshaped its form. Under
the eye of a blood red moon, the Dragon and Ennoia found
themselves in a sɷalemate. Recognising the futility of their
conﬂicɷ, they struck an ancient pact: Once per century, or in
the direst of times, their progeny would come to each other's
aid, a bond forged in the recognition of their shared, ineˁable
persistence.

XV aethon'sloyalty

XVIII dobrul'smight

ŀere was a Gangrel named Aethon Whisperwind, bound to
the wild, trusted his winged kin above all. But when darkness
stormed, it was not the beast but man who stood steadɶast in
the maelstrom's eye. ŀe ɶalcon, a creature of the skies,
vanished into the tempest's shroud. ŀus was it revealed: True
loyalty, a rare gem, may emerge from the most unforeseen of
realms.

Whispered in the night of the steppe is the ɷale of Dobrul the
Brave, the ﬁrst of the Anda, who slumbered deep beneath the
earth, his spirit entwined with the ancient victories of the
once great Mongolian horde. It is said that in his dependence
on their mighɷ, he lost his own, becoming but a ghostly echo
of their conquest as their accomplishment became echoes of
the pasɷ. And so he sleeps, and the land trembles with his
dreams, for it is foretold that he will rise once more at the end
of days, his awakening heralding an era of turmoil and blood.
So, the children of the night waiɷ, their hearts a stilled
drumbeaɷ, for the moment when Dobrul shall emerge, a tiɷan
reborn from the ashes of history.

XVI sylvana'shaligtree

Sylvana, priestess of Ennoia, erected a majestic monument in
honor of Ennoia and her descendants. In a profound act of
devotion, she severed her own hearɷ, letting her blood nourish
the soil. From this sacriﬁce, a mighty oak emerged, a towering
emblem of Gangrel fortitude and mighɷ. Yeɷ, others, envious
of its grandeur, conspired in the dark. ŀrough sorcery and
plotting they spawned a monstrous ivy that coiled insidiously
around the oak, sapping its strength and twisting it to their
own purpose. But it is said the Gangrel will be above all,
when the heart of Sylvana may beat freely once more.

XIX jukashthebreaker

Jukash, breaker of the gate of heaven, crusher of stone and
enemy of civilisation, tore down the cities of corruption in his
quest to restore purity. His might was uncontesɷable, until the
denizen of a cursed decadent city did invite him into their
labyrinth of stone and sin, wherein the Gangrel, children of
wilderness had their strength sapped. In their quest to cleanse
corruption, they ﬁnd themselves ensnared, for the city's
darkness is as intrinsic as roots to the earth. As they delve
deeper, their nature, once wild and free, becomes shackled to
the very corruption they sought to destroy, a tragic irony
woven into the ɶabric of the city's hearɷ. His descendant to
this day entwined in an eternal dance with the city, a dance
of predator and prey, forever bound to its shadowed streets.

XVII lysander'skin

Lysander, son of Ennoia and brother to wolves, was a warrior
of great renown and wisdom, his mind and heart open to all.
However he did not see the envious look of man. ŀey trapped
him with ﬁre and bounded his heart with wood. But in his
anguished howl, a lament for trust broken, did awaken the
vengeful spirits of the foresɷ. With eyes aﬂame and hearts
cold, the wolves, his true kin, emerged. ŀeir wrathful
retribution, a tragic ode to the shattered bond between man
and beasɷ.

XX themotherofbeasts

ŀe sun will grow black and the earth shall shudder, as the
children of blood and moon unite under their mother. As she
makes her war, the walls of man shall no longer sɷand ɷall
and their master’s will be cast down before her judgmenɷ.
